# 📦 Historical Archive

> **Historical archive** — older documents from the project's early stages, not assigned to a specific version. Contains 4 sub-folders: `research/` (empty — most research moved to `shared/research/`), `plans/` (2 old plans: fix-and-improve plan, display style transition plan), `reports/` (3 old reports: audit report, software analysis, AI chats summary), `qa/` (old checklist). Represents the project's early stage before the split into three versions.

← [Back to Home](EN-Home)
